ONE-ARMED ROBBER: CONSOLE COMMANDS THAT WORK
==============================================================================
Game build : Unreal Engine 4.27 shipping (OAR-Win64-Shipping.exe, 2026-08-23)
Game files : the Data Center heist update of 2026-10-01 (the exe did not change)
Console    : opened with ~ once the OARCommands installer (UE4SS) is in, or with UUU
Built from : a UUU object + console variable dump, the game's exe and its pak files
Commands are not case-sensitive. Spawning is in OAR_Spawn_Commands.txt.

HOW 'WORKS' WAS DECIDED
  * Cheat/exec commands: each one's code was read out of the running game.
    The ones that are empty in this build are listed in section 5 (DOES NOTHING).
  * Console variables + commands (sections 6 and 7): everything the game registered,
    minus the one Cheat-flagged cvar (blocked in shipping) and 13 'Unregistered' ini
    leftovers that do nothing.
  * Gameplay cheats only really apply when you are HOST or SOLO. As a client the
    server overrides you: god won't stop damage, ghost rubber-bands, summons are only
    on your screen.
  * Cheats need a cheat manager. The OARCommands install creates one for you; without it,
    type  EnableCheats  (works in solo play only).
  * The mod adds: bind / unbind / unbindall, summon <name> [count], summonstop, dupe [count],
    setmoney / addmoney / setlevel / setxp / maxskills / unlockall, noclip, revive,
    selectmap / forcemap, commandsharing / host, reloadconfig. All of their values and code are in
    Mods\OARCommands\config.lua, which you can edit. See the project README.

QUICK START
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  god                              invincible
  ghost / fly / walk               through walls / no gravity (no up or down in this game) / normal
  teleport                         go to where you are aiming
  slomo 0.3                        slow motion (slomo 1 = normal)
  summon goldbar 5                 spawn things (mod; full list: OAR_Spawn_Commands.txt)
  summon mona lisa                 an object: one class with its own look and values (mod; OBJECTS in OAR_Spawn_Commands.txt)
  dupe                             copy what you are looking at, with its look and values (mod)
  bind x destroytarget             put any command on a key (mod; the game's own SetBind is dead)
  setmoney 5000000                 set your cash and save it (mod; also addmoney, setlevel, setxp)
  maxskills / unlockall            every skill at top tier / every cash-bought weapon, mod, tool, armor (mod)
  noclip                           fly through walls: WASD, Space up, Ctrl down, Shift faster; again to land (mod)
  revive                           get back up with full health (mod)
  selectmap museum                 host: pick the heist from the console; selectmap alone lists them (mod)
  forcemap museum                  host: start a map now for everyone, no ready-up or countdown (mod)
  reloadconfig                     load Mods\OARCommands\config.lua again after editing it: every command's values and code (mod)
  commandsharing 1                 host: guests with the mod can run commands through your game (mod; 0-3)
  DestroyTarget                    delete what you are looking at
  DestroyAll NPC_Police_base_C     delete every cop on the map
  PlayersOnly                      freeze all AI and physics (again to unfreeze)
  ToggleDebugCamera                free camera (again to exit)
  ChangeSize 3                     giant robber (ChangeSize 1 = normal)
  stat fps                         FPS counter
  open CheatLevel                  load a leftover dev map called CheatLevel (solo, untested)


1. CHEATS (CheatManager)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  God                                          Toggle invincibility
  Fly                                          No gravity only: you hover at your height, cannot go up or down (walk to stop)
  Ghost                                        Like fly plus no collision: walk through walls at your height (walk to stop)
  Walk                                         Back to normal walking after fly/ghost
  teleport                                     Teleport to whatever your crosshair is pointing at
  Summon <ClassName:text>                      Spawn an actor in front of you. See OAR_Spawn_Commands.txt
  Slomo <NewTimeDilation:number>               Game speed. 1 = normal, 0.5 = half, 3 = triple
  ChangeSize <F:number>                        Scale your character. 1 = normal, 2 = double, 0.5 = half
  DestroyTarget                                Delete the thing you are looking at
  DamageTarget <DamageAmount:number>           Damage the thing you are looking at by this amount
  DestroyAll <aClass:class>                    Delete every actor of a class, e.g. DestroyAll NPC_Police_base_C
  DestroyPawns <aClass:class>                  Delete every non-player pawn of a class, e.g. DestroyPawns NPC_Guard_C
  DestroyAllPawnsExceptTarget                  Delete every AI pawn except the one you look at
  PlayersOnly                                  Freeze everything except players (AI, physics). Run again to undo
  FreezeFrame <Delay:number>                   Pause the game after this many seconds
  ToggleDebugCamera                            Detached free camera; your character stays put. Run again to exit
  ViewSelf                                     Camera back to your own character
  ViewPlayer <S:text>                          Spectate another player by name
  ViewActor <ActorName:name>                   Put the camera on a named actor (names are in the objects dump)
  ViewClass <DesiredClass:class>               Cycle the camera through actors of a class, e.g. ViewClass NPC_Guard_C
  BugIt <ScreenShotDescription:text>           Log your exact position/rotation (BugItGo line) to the log
  BugItGo <X:number> <Y:number> <Z:number> <Pitch:number> <Yaw:number> <Roll:number>
                                               Teleport to coordinates: BugItGo X Y Z Pitch Yaw Roll
  LogLoc                                       Print your location to the log
  StreamLevelIn <PackageName:name>             Stream a sub-level in by package name
  OnlyLoadLevel <PackageName:name>             Load a streaming sub-level without making it visible
  StreamLevelOut <PackageName:name>            Stream a sub-level out by package name
  InvertMouse                                  Toggle inverted mouse Y
  SetMouseSensitivityToDefault                 Reset mouse sensitivity
  FlushLog                                     Write the log to disk now
  CheatScript <ScriptName:text>                Run a [CheatScript.Name] command list from the game ini
  SetWorldOrigin                               Rebase the world origin to your position (debug)
  UpdateSafeArea                               Refresh the UI safe-zone (debug)
  DumpChatState                                Log chat state (debug)
  DumpOnlineSessionState                       Log online session state (debug)
  DumpPartyState                               Log party state (debug)
  DumpVoiceMutingState                         Log voice mute state (debug)
  SpawnServerStatReplicator                    Net stats replicator (debug)
  DestroyServerStatReplicator                  Net stats replicator (debug)
  ToggleServerStatReplicatorClientOverwrite    Net stats replicator (debug)
  ToggleServerStatReplicatorUpdateStatNet      Net stats replicator (debug)
  DebugCapsuleSweep                            Draw a debug capsule trace from your view (debug)
  DebugCapsuleSweepSize <HalfHeight:number> <Radius:number>
                                               Set debug capsule size: HalfHeight Radius
  DebugCapsuleSweepChannel <Channel:number>    Set debug capsule trace channel
  DebugCapsuleSweepComplex <bTraceComplex:0/1> Toggle complex collision for debug capsule
  DebugCapsuleSweepCapture                     Capture the current debug capsule trace
  DebugCapsuleSweepPawn                        Toggle continuous debug capsule sweep from your pawn
  DebugCapsuleSweepClear                       Clear debug capsule drawings
  BugItStringCreator <ViewLocation:struct> <ViewRotation:struct> <GoString:text> <LocString:text>
                                               Helper used by BugIt (no visible effect)

2. OTHER COMMANDS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  [PLAYER CONTROLLER]
  EnableCheats                                 Create the cheat manager if god/fly/summon say 'Command not recognized'
  FOV <NewFOV:number>                          Set field of view, e.g. FOV 110 (the game may reset it)
  Pause                                        Pause / unpause
  RestartLevel                                 Reload the current map (host)
  LocalTravel <URL:text>                       Travel to a map URL locally
  SwitchLevel <URL:text>                       Switch to a map URL
  SetName <S:text>                             Change your player name
  Camera <NewMode:name>                        Camera style: Camera FreeCam / ThirdPerson / FirstPerson / Default (game camera may override)
  ToggleSpeaking <bInSpeaking:0/1>             Toggle voice chat transmit
  StartFire <FireModeNum:number>               Fire the current weapon
  ConsoleKey <Key:struct>                      Simulate a key press by name
  ServerExec <Msg:text>                        Ask the server to run a command (host must allow it)
  TestServerLevelVisibilityChange <PackageName:name> <Filename:name>
                                               Net debug

  [AI]
  AIIgnorePlayers                              Engine 'AI ignores players' toggle. The game's own guard logic may not check it
  AILoggingVerbose                             Verbose AI logging

  [INPUT / KEYBINDS]
  SetBind <BindName:name> <Command:text>       Saves a key bind that this shipping build never runs. Use the mod's bind instead
  SetMouseSensitivity <Sensitivity:number>     Set mouse sensitivity
  InvertAxis <AxisName:name>                   Invert an input axis by name
  InvertAxisKey <AxisKey:struct>               Invert an input axis key
  ClearSmoothing                               Reset mouse smoothing

  [HUD / DEBUG OVERLAY]
  ShowHUD                                      Toggle the engine HUD (the game's own UI widgets may stay)
  ShowDebug <DebugType:name>                   Debug overlay: ShowDebug, ShowDebug Camera, ShowDebug Collision, ShowDebug Physics
  NextDebugTarget                              Next ShowDebug target
  PreviousDebugTarget                          Previous ShowDebug target
  ShowDebugToggleSubCategory <Category:name>   Toggle a ShowDebug sub-category
  ShowDebugForReticleTargetToggle <DesiredClass:class>
                                               ShowDebug whatever is under the crosshair

  [VIEWPORT]
  ShowTitleSafeArea                            Draw the TV safe-area frame

  [DEBUG CAMERA (only while ToggleDebugCamera is on)]
  ShowDebugSelectedInfo                        In debug camera: show info on the selected actor

3. ENGINE TEXT COMMANDS  (complete: every command word compiled into this game's exe)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  [MAPS AND NETWORK]
  open <map>                                   Load a map (solo). Add ?listen to host it: open Casino?listen
  travel <map>                                 Travel to a map
  servertravel <map>                           Host only: move everyone to a map
  streammap <map>                              Load a map with streaming
  disconnect                                   Leave the current game
  reconnect                                    Rejoin the last server
  cancelasyncload                              Stop background loading

  [SCREEN, QUALITY AND PICTURES]
  setres 1920x1080f                            Resolution + mode: f fullscreen, w windowed, wf borderless
  fullscreen                                   Toggle fullscreen (also toggle_fullscreen, forcefullscreen)
  gamma 2.2                                    Display gamma
  scalability 0                                All quality settings at once: 0 low, 1 medium, 2 high, 3 epic, auto
  viewmode unlit                               lit, unlit, wireframe, lightingonly (debug modes are blocked)
  nextviewmode / prevviewmode                  Cycle view modes
  show collision                               Toggle an engine show flag: show fog, show particles, show bounds...
  showlayer <layer>                            Toggle a named actor layer
  HighResShot 2                                Screenshot at 2x (or HighResShot 3840x2160), saved in OAR\Saved\Screenshots
  HighResShotUI                                High-res screenshot settings window
  shot                                         Screenshot (also: screenshot)
  bugscreenshot <name>                         Named screenshot (bugscreenshotwithhudinfo adds HUD info)
  killparticles                                Remove every particle effect in the world
  forceskellod lod=1                           Force character mesh level of detail (lod=0 to reset)
  pauserenderclock                             Freeze material animations
  showui                                       Toggle viewport UI
  r.ResetViewState                             Reset temporal rendering history
  r.LockView                                   Lock the rendering view (debug)
  togglemipfade / texturedefrag / precache     Texture and loading internals

  [INSPECT AND CHANGE OBJECTS  (class names come from OAR_Full_Object_List.txt)]
  getall <Class> <Property>                    Print a property on every object of a class
  get <Class> <Property>                       Print a class default value
  set <Class> <Property> <Value>               Change a property on every object of a class and its default
  setnopec <Class> <Property> <Value>          Same as set, without change notifications
  listprops <Class> *                          List a class's properties (wildcards allowed)
  showdefaults <Class>                         Print a class's default values
  display <Object> <Property>                  Show one object's property on screen
  displayall <Class> <Property>                Show a property for every object of a class on screen
  displayalllocation <Class>                   Show every object's location (displayallrotation too)
  displayclear                                 Remove the display/displayall overlays
  getalllocation <Class>                       Print every object's location (getallrotation too)
  reattachcomponents <Class>                   Re-attach components (reregistercomponents too)
  logactorcounts                               Log how many actors each level has
  listpawncomponents                           Log every pawn's components
  listskelmeshes                               Log every skeletal mesh
  listawakebodies                              Log awake physics bodies (listsimbodies, listmovebody too)
  showpendingkills                             Log objects waiting to be deleted

  [RUNNING COMMANDS]
  exec <file.txt>                              Run each line of a text file as a command. Use a full path: exec C:\cmds.txt (untested)
  defer <command>                              Run a command on the next frame
  ce <EventName>                               Fire a level-script event (few maps have any; see the object list)
  togglecvar <cvar> <a> <b>                    Flip a setting between two values: togglecvar t.MaxFPS 30 0

  [REPLAYS]
  demorec <name>                               Start recording a replay
  demostop                                     Stop recording or playback
  demoplay <name>                              Play a recorded replay
  demopause                                    Pause replay playback
  demoscrub <seconds>                          Jump to a time in the replay
  demospeed <rate>                             Replay speed
  stopmoviecapture                             Stop a movie capture

  [INFO AND LOGS]
  stat fps                                     Overlays: fps, unit, unitgraph, detailed, hitches, levels, summary, ai, none
  gamever                                      Print the build version (also gameversion)
  log list                                     List log categories; log <category> <verbosity> changes one
  flushlog                                     Write the log file now
  dumpticks                                    Log everything that ticks (grouped / enabled / disabled)
  getmaxtickrate                               Print the tick rate cap
  flushpersistentdebuglines                    Clear debug lines drawn on screen
  crackurl <url>                               Print how the engine parses a travel URL
  movecomptimes                                Log movement component timings

  [PHYSICS DEBUG]
  pxvis collision                              PhysX debug drawing (apexvis, physxinfo, physxshared, pvd, prephysbones)
  physx_clear_all                              Clear PhysX debug state

  [ONLINE, STEAM AND NETWORK INTERNALS  (developer dumps; safe but not useful in play)]
  online ...                                   Online subsystem: friend, presence, session, auth, synclobbies, receipts, voice
  net ...                                      Network driver commands
  sockets ...                                  Socket subsystem
  http test / http dumpreq                     HTTP module
  ssl ...                                      SSL module
  tcpmessaging status                          Messaging transports (udpmessaging, messaging)
  replaystreamer ...                           Replay streaming
  media ...                                    Media player
  webcall / dumpembedded / exitembedded        Embedded browser bridge
  hmd / stereo on / vr.SetTrackingOrigin       VR headset commands (hmdpos, hmdversion, vr.mirrormode)

  [DO NOT USE]
  deletecloudfiles                             Deletes cloud files. This game keeps your cash and progress in Steam Cloud
  DoubleFreeFinderCrash                        Crashes the game on purpose
  purchase ...                                 Store purchase test hook
  MallocFrameProfiler                          Heavy memory profiler
  exit / quit                                  Closes the game

  [ALSO RECOGNISED  (sub-options of the commands above, e.g. stat budget, log list)]
    add  block  budget  cancel  cancelmatinee  close
    connect  customloddata  deny  denyall  detailed  disable
    disabled  dump  dumpblocked  dumprecent  empty  enable
    enabled  flush  grouped  hide  list  nodebug
    off  pausereceive  queryrecent  remove  removeindex  restart
    setname  setowner  shutdown  status  test  togglestreamingvolumes
    vcbitrate  vccomplexity  vcdump  vcvbr

  Maps in the game's pak (open <name> in solo, servertravel <name> as host).

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  t.MaxFPS                           = 165.0    Caps FPS to the given value.  Set to <= 0 to be uncapped.
  r.ScreenPercentage                 = 100.0    To render in lower resolution and upscale for better performance (combined up with the blenable post proces...
  r.VSync                            = 0        0: VSync is disabled.(default)

  r.DepthOfFieldQuality              = 2        Allows to adjust the depth of field quality. Currently only fully affects BokehDOF. GaussianDOF is either 0...
  r.Fog                              = 1        0: disabled

  r.Tonemapper.Sharpen               = 0.0      Sharpening in the tonemapper (not for mobile), actual implementation is work in progress, clamped at 10
  r.DefaultFeature.AntiAliasing      = 2        Engine default (project setting) for AntiAliasingMethod is (postprocess volume/camera/game setting still ca...
  r.PostProcessAAQuality             = 4        Defines the postprocess anti aliasing method which allows to adjust for quality or performance.
  r.BloomQuality                     = 5        0: off, no performance impact.
  r.LensFlareQuality                 = 2        0: off but best for performance
  r.SceneColorFringeQuality          = 1        0: off but best for performance
  r.EyeAdaptationQuality             = 2        Defines the eye adaptation quality which allows to adjust for quality or performance.
  r.Shadow.MaxResolution             = 2048     Max square dimensions (in texels) allowed for rendering shadow depths. Range 4 to hardware limit. Higher = ...
  r.SSR.Quality                      = 3        Whether to use screen space reflections and at what quality setting.
  r.AmbientOcclusionLevels           = -1       Defines how many mip levels are using during the ambient occlusion calculation. This is useful when tweakin...
  r.MipMapLODBias                    = 0.0      Apply additional mip map bias for all 2D textures, range of -15.0 to 15.0
  r.StaticMeshLODDistanceScale       = 1.0      Scale factor for the distance used in computing discrete LOD for static meshes. (defaults to 1)
  r.Streaming.PoolSize               = 1000     -1: Default texture pool size, otherwise the size in MB
  foliage.LODDistanceScale           = 1.0      Scale factor for the distance used in computing LOD for foliage.
  sg.ViewDistanceQuality             = 3        Scalability quality state (internally used by scalability system, ini load/save or using SCALABILITY consol...

  sg.PostProcessQuality              = 3        Scalability quality state (internally used by scalability system, ini load/save or using SCALABILITY consol...
  sg.AntiAliasingQuality             = 3        Scalability quality state (internally used by scalability system, ini load/save or using SCALABILITY consol...
  r.SetNearClipPlane                            Set the near clipping plane (in cm)
  ShowFlag.Fog                                  Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.PostProcessing                       Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.MotionBlur                           Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.DepthOfField                         Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.Vignette                             Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.Bloom                                Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.Decals                               Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...
  ShowFlag.Particles                            Allows to override a specific showflag (works in editor and game, "show" only works in game and UI only in ...

5. DOES NOTHING IN THIS BUILD (compiled out or not reachable)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  DebugRemovePlayer                            empty function in shipping build
  DebugCreatePlayer                            empty function in shipping build
  SetBandwidthLimit                            empty function in shipping build
  Say                                          the game mode (RobberGM_C) is a GameModeBase, not a GameMode
  SendToConsole                                empty function in shipping build
  ToggleAILogging                              empty function in shipping build
  TestCollisionDistance                        empty function in shipping build
  SSSwapControllers                            empty function in shipping build
  SetConsoleTarget                             empty function in shipping build
  StopPerformanceSnapshots                     function library, not in the console's command chain
  StartPerformanceSnapshots                    function library, not in the console's command chain
  LogPerformanceSnapshot                       function library, not in the console's command chain
  obj ... / ke ... / help / DumpConsoleCommands
                                               not compiled into this exe
  viewmode shadercomplexity (and other debug view modes)
                                               blocked in shipping
  r.Shaders.SkipCompression                    Cheat-flagged cvar, blocked in shipping

  Registered but best left alone:
    c.ToggleGPUCrashedFlagDbg                  tells the engine the GPU crashed
    CreateDummyFileInPersistentStorage         writes junk to disk
    r.d3d11.dumpliveobjects                    D3D11 only, game runs D3D12/D3D11 debug
    r.RecompileRenderer                        long hitch
    ReloadGlobalShaders                        long hitch / possible crash
    ShrinkUObjectHashTables                    hitch
    SynthBenchmark                             runs a benchmark and changes quality settings
    net.SimulateConnections                    spawns fake network clients
    Net.CreateBandwidthGenerator               floods the network
    Net.GenerateConstantBandwidth              floods the network
    Net.GeneratePeriodicBandwidthSpike         floods the network

    PersistentStorageCategoryStats                       Get the stat of each persistent storage stats
    ReloadGlobalShaders                                  Reloads the global shaders file
    SetThreadAffinity                                    Sets the thread affinity. A single arg of default resets the thread affinity, otherwise pairs of ...
    ShrinkUObjectHashTables                              Shrinks all of the UObject hash tables.
    SparseDelegateReport                                 Outputs a report of what sparse delegates are bound. SparseDelegateReport [name=<ObjectName>] [de...
    SynthBenchmark                                       Run simple benchmark to get some metrics to find reasonable game settings automatically
    ToggleForceDefaultMaterial                           Render all meshes with the default material.
    ToggleLight                                          Toggles all lights whose name contains the specified string
    ToggleLightmapPreview                                Toggles lightmap preview in editor
    ToggleReversedIndexBuffers                           Render static meshes with negative transform determinants using a reversed index buffer.
    ToggleShadowIndexBuffers                             Render static meshes with an optimized shadow index buffer that minimizes unique vertices.

    vm.UseOptimizedVMByteCode = 1 [Int32]  If > 0 optimized vector VM code will be excuted at runtime.
